# Skills · G · part 102

936 entries, grill-me to grill-me-idea. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **grill-me**: Interrogate the user one question at a time — to test whether they actually understand something, or to extract a requirement they have not stated clearly. Use when the user says "grill me", "quiz me", "test me", "poke holes in this", "do I actually get it?", "grill me until you understand", or asks to be interrogated before acting on something ambiguous. ([Capicuaman/agent-vault](https://github.com/Capicuaman/agent-vault/tree/HEAD/skills/general/grill-me) · ★1)
- **grill-me**: Stress-test a plan or design through relentless questioning until every branch of the decision tree is resolved. Walks dependencies between decisions, provides recommendations, and explores the codebase to answer questions directly. Use when user wants to stress-test a plan, get grilled on their design, or mentions "grill me". ([chiruu12/nudge](https://github.com/chiruu12/nudge/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/grill-me) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **grill-me**: Trigger: grill me, challenge my plan, sharpen design. Run a relentless interview to expose assumptions and improve a plan. ([ChitoLabs/ai-skill-pack](https://github.com/ChitoLabs/ai-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-me) · ★1)
- **grill-me**: Stress-test a goal harness, goal card, plan, or proposed next action by finding concrete failure modes, hidden assumptions, weak success criteria, scope creep, stale-doc risk, rabbit-hole loops, missing constraints, and undocumented product/scope defaults. Use after /goal or before committing to an execution path. ([chokevin/kstack](https://github.com/chokevin/kstack/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-me) · ★1)
- **grill-me**: Interview the user about every aspect of their task until a shared understanding is reached. Use this skill when the user types /grill-me or says "grill me", "interview me", "ask me questions first", "walk me through the design", or any time the task is ambiguous and needs to be fully scoped before work begins. Walk down each branch of the design tree, resolving dependencies between decisions one-by-one, providing a recommended answer for each question. ([cicorias/skills](https://github.com/cicorias/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-me) · ★1)
- **grill-me**: Run a relentless one-question-at-a-time interview on a sketch or plan until every branch of the decision tree resolves into either a decision, a rabbit hole, or an explicit no-go. Use when the user wants to stress-test a fat-marker sketch before any code is written, or asks to be "grilled", "interviewed", or to "stress-test" a plan. Adapted from Matt Pocock's /grill-me (mattpocock/skills) and combined with Shape Up's appetite + fidelity discipline. ([CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops](https://github.com/CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-me) · ★1)

- **grill-me**: 把 AI 从"好好先生"变"拷问官"。当用户提出方案、需求、选题、架构或功能想法并希望先对齐再动手时使用。触发场景与词语：用户说"grill me"、"拷问一下"、"先挑毛病"、"先当杠精"、"先别写，先问"、"帮我审审这个方案/需求"、"跟 AI 对方案"；或任务涉及写功能、改架构、定选题、讨论方案，且关键假设、依赖、边界或验收标准尚未对齐。核心行为：先提问后输出，顺决策树挖依赖/边界/验收标准/隐藏假设，可查证的自己查，每问附推荐答法，挑完反例再下结论。不适用于：用户要的是现成的提示词模板/话术参考（走 `prompt-library-40`）。不适用于：需要跨经典交叉研判的人生/商业综合决策分析（走 `distill-cat`）。 ([luotiandong799-coder/AI-Skills](https://github.com/luotiandong799-coder/AI-Skills/tree/HEAD/agent/grill-me) · ★1)
